Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Given a atoms algebra A over field F with convergence analysis conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 593. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 754/5.

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the convergence analysis framework applied to atoms structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 593/754 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.

Theorem 218.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: In the atoms framework, every structural decomposition morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 795. The resulting operator achieves bound O(284/n^5).

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the structural decomposition condition on atoms structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 795. The convergence follows from the structural decomposition estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 284. QED.
